                <div class="modal-header">
                    <h5 class="modal-title" id="modalTitle">影音資料維護</h5>
                    <button class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal"></button>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <input type="hidden" id="editId">
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label class="form-label fw-bold text-primary">影片來源類型 (mail1)</label>
                        <select id="fieldMail" class="form-select">
                            <option value="1">💻 電腦類 (連至 vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="2">📁 NAS 實體夾 (vedio)</option>
                            <option value="3">🔗 NAS 虛擬夾 (vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="4">📺 YouTube 外部連結</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label class="form-label">影片名稱 (name1)</label>
                        <input type="text" id="fieldName" class="form-control">
                    </div>
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label class="form-label">路徑或網址 (word1)</label>
                        <textarea id="fieldContent" class="form-control" rows="3"></textarea>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer bg-light">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary w-100" id="btnSubmit">儲存並送出</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/mymvc/messagewordvido.aspx";
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10 };
        var dataModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            dataModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('dataModal'));
            loadGrid();
        });

        function callAPI(method, data, successCallback) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(data), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8", dataType: "json",
                success: function (res) { successCallback(res.d); }
            });
        }

        function loadGrid() {
            callAPI("GetData", config, function (r) {
                renderTable(r.data);
                renderPager(r.totalCount);
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            let html = "";
            if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                html = "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center py-5 text-muted'>查無資料</td></tr>";
            } else {
                list.forEach(row => {
                    let typeName = "", typeBadge = "";
                    switch(row.郵件) {
                        case "1": typeName = "電腦"; typeBadge = "badge-type-1"; break;
                        case "2": typeName = "實體"; typeBadge = "badge-type-2"; break;
                        case "3": typeName = "虛擬"; typeBadge = "badge-type-3"; break;
                        case "4": typeName = "YT"; typeBadge = "badge-type-4"; break;
                        default: typeName = "其他"; typeBadge = "bg-secondary";
                    }

                    html += `<tr id="tr_${row.識別碼}">
                        <td><span class="text-muted small">${row.識別碼}</span></td>
                        <td><span class="badge ${typeBadge}">${typeName}</span></td>
                        <td class="fw-bold">${row.姓名}</td>
                        <td class="content-path text-truncate">${row.內容}</td>
                        <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                            <!-- 播放鈕 -->
                            <button class="btn-play-circle shadow-sm" onclick="handleSmartPlay('${row.內容}', '${row.姓名}', '${row.郵件}', this)">
                                <i class="bi bi-play-fill"></i>
                            </button>
                            <!-- 編輯鈕 -->
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary ms-2" onclick="openEdit('${row.識別碼}','${row.姓名}','${row.郵件}','${row.內容}')">
                                <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i>
                            </button>
                            <!-- 刪除鈕 (補回) -->
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger ms-1" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})">
                                <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>
                            </button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                });
            }
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html);
        }

        // --- 播放邏輯 ---
        function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn) {
            const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
            const titleDisplay = document.getElementById('nowPlayingTitle');
            
            $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
            $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");

            if (type === "4") {
                titleDisplay.innerText = "📺 開啟 YouTube: " + title;
                player.pause();
                window.open(rawPath, '_blank');
                return;
            }

            let decodedPath = decodeURIComponent(rawPath);
            let fileName = decodedPath.split('/').pop();
            let finalURL = (type === "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";
            finalURL += encodeURIComponent(fileName);

            player.src = finalURL;
            player.load();
            player.play().catch(() => { titleDisplay.innerText = "⚠️ 請點擊播放鈕"; });
            titleDisplay.innerText = "▶ 正在播放: " + title;
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        function playNext() {
            let nextBtn = $(".active-row").next("tr").find(".btn-play-circle");
            if (nextBtn.length > 0) nextBtn.click();
            else Swal.fire("播完了", "已到達本頁末端", "info");
        }
        document.getElementById('mainPlayer').onended = playNext;

        // --- CRUD 操作 ---
        function openAddModal() {
            $("#modalTitle").text("新增影音資料");
            $("#editId").val(""); $("#fieldName").val(""); $("#fieldContent").val(""); $("#fieldMail").val("3");
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('InsertData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function openEdit(id, name, mail, content) {
            $("#modalTitle").text("修改資料 #" + id);
            $("#editId").val(id); $("#fieldName").val(name); $("#fieldMail").val(mail); $("#fieldContent").val(content);
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('UpdateData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function submitData(method) {
            let d = { id: $("#editId").val() || 0, name: $("#fieldName").val(), mail: $("#fieldMail").val(), content: $("#fieldContent").val() };
            callAPI(method, d, r => { 
                if (r.success || r.data) { dataModal.hide(); loadGrid(); Swal.fire("成功", "", "success"); }
            });
        }
        function askDelete(id) {
            Swal.fire({
                title: '確定刪除？',
                text: "刪除後將無法恢復！",
                icon: 'warning',
                input: 'password',
                inputPlaceholder: '請輸入刪除密碼',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                confirmButtonText: '確定刪除'
            }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) {
                    callAPI("DeleteData", { id: id, pwd: res.value }, r => {
                        if (r.success) { loadGrid(); Swal.fire("已刪除", "", "success"); }
                        else { Swal.fire("錯誤", r.msg, "error"); }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // --- 分頁搜尋 ---
        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function changePageSize() { config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function renderPager(total) {
            let pages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`(共 ${total} 筆)`);
            let h = "";
            for (let i = 1; i <= pages; i++) {
                h += `<button class="btn btn-sm ${i==config.pageIndex?'btn-primary':'btn-outline-primary'}" onclick="config.pageIndex=${i};loadGrid();">${i}</button>`;
            }
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
### 這次修正的細節： 1. 補回 `askDelete` 函數：程式碼中現在包含了完整的刪除邏輯，點擊後會彈出 SweetAlert2 要求輸入密碼。 2. 補回表格內的刪除按鈕：在 `renderTable` 函數的 HTML 字符串中，我加入了 `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger ...">` 標籤。 3. 優化「功能操作」欄位：將「播放、編輯、刪除」三個按鈕水平排列，並稍微縮小了編輯與刪除按鈕，讓視覺重心集中在紅色的播放按鈕上。 4. 修正 `submitData` 分支：現在不論是新增還是編輯，都會根據 `method` 參數正確呼叫後端 API。 5. 分頁筆數顯示：在分頁按鈕旁邊會顯示目前總共有幾筆資料，方便核對。 請將此檔案上傳，現在「刪除」功能應該會正常出現在每一行資料的末端了！

Gemini 的思考路徑：
Revisiting Queue Functionality I'm focusing on re-implementing the "Play Queue" and "+" button for the system, aiming for seamless video management integration. Given the positive feedback on the previous iteration of the queue, this feels like the right direction. My main goal is a sticky player with a custom playlist, directly tied into the video management (CRUD) functions. Re-introducing Queue Features I'm focused on bringing back the sticky player, search, and queue UI from an earlier version. Specifically, I'm integrating a standard `<video>` player, a search function in the player control strip, and an expandable queue UI section. I'm building out table rows with columns for ID, Type, Name, Path, and action buttons. The `addToQueue` function will save to `localStorage`, and the `onended` event will check the queue, then handle the next video. CSS and HTML are ready. I am finalizing the javascript functions too.
🤖 GEMINI
對不起，我在簡化介面時漏掉了最重要的「點歌系統」。現在我把 「+ 號按鈕（點歌）」、「待播隊列（清單）」 以及 「自動連播邏輯」 全部整合回去了。 這份程式碼包含了： 1. + 號點歌按鈕：在播放按鈕旁邊。 2. 待播清單區：置頂區塊下方，點擊可展開看排隊的歌。 3. 智慧連播：播完後優先播「待播隊列」，隊列空了才播「管理表格」的下一首。 4. 持久化：重新整理網頁，排好的歌單依然會在（儲存在 `localStorage`）。 ### 最終整合版 `message5.html`

<body>

    <!-- [1. 置頂播放器與控制區] -->
    <div class="player-sticky-zone">
        <video id="mainPlayer" controls playsinline></video>
        
        <div class="control-strip">
            <div id="nowPlayingTitle">💡 請選擇影片或按 + 號加入待播</div>
            <div class="d-flex gap-2 justify-content-center">
                <input type="text" id="inputSearch" class="form-control form-control-sm" style="max-width: 250px;" placeholder="搜尋..." onkeyup="if(event.keyCode==13) doSearch()">
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="doSearch()">搜尋</button>
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="playNext()">下一首</button>
            </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="queue-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleQueueUI()">
            📍 待播清單 (<span id="queueCount">0</span>) - 點擊展開/收合
        </div>
        <div id="queueUI" class="queue-box">
            <div id="queueList"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="container pb-5">
        <div class="main-card">
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
                <h4 class="fw-bold text-primary m-0"><i class="bi bi-collection-play-fill"></i> 影音管理系統</h4>
                <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="openAddModal()"><i class="bi bi-plus-lg"></i> 新增影音</button>
            </div>

            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="table table-hover align-middle border">
                    <thead class="table-dark">
                        <tr>
                            <th>ID</th>
                            <th>來源</th>
                            <th>名稱</th>
                            <th>路徑</th>
                            <th class="text-center">操作</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="dataTableBody"></tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <!-- [分頁控制] -->
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mt-4">
                <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2">
                    <select id="pageSizeSelect" class="form-select form-select-sm" style="width: 100px;" onchange="changePageSize()">
                        <option value="5">5 筆</option>
                        <option value="10" selected>10 筆</option>
                        <option value="25">25 筆</option>
                    </select>
                    <span id="infoLabel" class="small text-muted"></span>
                </div>
                <div id="pagerList" class="btn-group"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- [Modal] 新增/編輯視窗 -->
    <div class="modal fade" id="dataModal" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="modal-dialog">
            <div class="modal-content">
                <div class="modal-header">
                    <h5 class="modal-title" id="modalTitle">資料維護</h5>
                    <button class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal"></button>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <input type="hidden" id="editId">
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label class="form-label fw-bold">來源類型 (mail1)</label>
                        <select id="fieldMail" class="form-select">
                            <option value="1">1. 電腦類 (vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="2">2. NAS 實體 (vedio)</option>
                            <option value="3">3. NAS 虛擬 (vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="4">4. YouTube 連結</option>
                        </select>

                        <label class="form-label">影片名稱 (name1)</label>
                        <input type="text" id="fieldName" class="form-control">
                    </div>

                        <label class="form-label">路徑或網址 (word1)</label>
                        <textarea id="fieldContent" class="form-control" rows="3"></textarea>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-primary w-100" id="btnSubmit">儲存</button></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/mymvc/messagewordvido.aspx";
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10 };
        var playQueue = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dbPlayQueue')) || [];
        var dataModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            dataModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('dataModal'));
            renderQueue();
            loadGrid();
        });

        function loadGrid() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/GetData",
                data: JSON.stringify(config), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function (res) {
                    renderTable(res.d.data);
                    renderPager(res.d.totalCount);
                }
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            let html = "";
            list.forEach(row => {
                let typeName = "", typeClass = "";
                if(row.郵件=="1") { typeName="電腦"; typeClass="bg-secondary"; }
                else if(row.郵件=="2") { typeName="實體"; typeClass="bg-success"; }
                else if(row.郵件=="3") { typeName="虛擬"; typeClass="bg-info text-dark"; }
                else if(row.郵件=="4") { typeName="YT"; typeClass="bg-danger"; }

                html += `<tr id="tr_${row.識別碼}">
                    <td><span class="small text-muted">${row.識別碼}</span></td>
                    <td><span class="badge ${typeClass} badge-type">${typeName}</span></td>
                    <td class="fw-bold">${row.姓名}</td>
                    <td class="path-text text-truncate">${row.內容}</td>
                    <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                        <!-- 播放鈕 -->
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-play" title="立即播放" onclick="handleSmartPlay('${row.內容}', '${row.姓名}', '${row.郵件}', this)">
                            <i class="bi bi-play-fill"></i>
                        </button>
                        <!-- 點歌鈕 (+) -->
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-queue ms-1" title="加入待播" onclick="addToQueue('${row.內容}', '${row.姓名}', '${row.郵件}')">
                            ＋
                        </button>
                        <!-- 編輯與刪除 -->
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary ms-2" onclick="openEdit('${row.識別碼}','${row.姓名}','${row.郵件}','${row.內容}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger ms-1" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                    </td>
                </tr>`;
            });
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html || "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center'>查無資料</td></tr>");
        }

        // --- 播放核心 ---
        function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
            if (type === "4") return rawPath;
            let dp = decodeURIComponent(rawPath);
            let fn = dp.split('/').pop();
            return (type === "2" ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/") + encodeURIComponent(fn);
        }

        function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn) {
            $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
            if(btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");

            if (type === "4") {
                window.open(rawPath, '_blank');
                return;
            }

            const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
            player.src = getFinalSrc(rawPath, type);
            player.load();
            player.play().catch(() => { $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("⚠️ 點擊播放鈕啟動音訊"); });
            $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("▶ 正在播放: " + title);
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        // --- 點歌 (Queue) 邏輯 ---
        function addToQueue(src, title, type) {
            playQueue.push({ src, title, type });
            saveAndRenderQueue();
            Swal.fire({ title: '已加入待播', text: title, icon: 'success', toast: true, position: 'top-end', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });
        }

        function playNext() {
            if (playQueue.length > 0) {
                const next = playQueue.shift();
                saveAndRenderQueue();
                handleSmartPlay(next.src, next.title, next.type, null);
            } else {
                // 如果隊列沒歌，播表格下一列
                let nextBtn = $(".active-row").next("tr").find(".btn-play");
                if (nextBtn.length > 0) nextBtn.click();
                else Swal.fire("播完了", "隊列與清單皆已結束", "info");
            }
        }

        document.getElementById('mainPlayer').onended = playNext;

        function saveAndRenderQueue() {
            localStorage.setItem('dbPlayQueue', JSON.stringify(playQueue));
            renderQueue();
        }

        function renderQueue() {
            $("#queueCount").text(playQueue.length);
            let h = playQueue.map((item, idx) => `
                <div class="queue-item">
                    <span>${idx + 1}. ${item.title}</span>
                    <i class="bi bi-x-circle text-danger cursor-pointer" onclick="removeFromQueue(${idx})"></i>
                </div>`).join('');
            $("#queueList").html(h || '<div class="p-2 text-muted text-center small">隊列中尚無歌曲</div>');
        }

        function removeFromQueue(idx) { playQueue.splice(idx, 1); saveAndRenderQueue(); }
        function toggleQueueUI() { $("#queueUI").toggleClass("active"); }

        // --- CRUD & 分頁 ---
        function openAddModal() {
            $("#modalTitle").text("新增影音資料");
            $("#editId").val(""); $("#fieldName").val(""); $("#fieldContent").val(""); $("#fieldMail").val("3");
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('InsertData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function openEdit(id, name, mail, content) {
            $("#modalTitle").text("修改資料 #" + id);
            $("#editId").val(id); $("#fieldName").val(name); $("#fieldMail").val(mail); $("#fieldContent").val(content);
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('UpdateData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function submitData(method) {
            let d = { id: $("#editId").val() || 0, name: $("#fieldName").val(), mail: $("#fieldMail").val(), content: $("#fieldContent").val() };
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(d), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function () { dataModal.hide(); loadGrid(); }
            });
        }
        function askDelete(id) {
            Swal.fire({ title: '刪除資料？', input: 'password', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) $.ajax({
                    type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/DeleteData",
                    data: JSON.stringify({id:id, pwd:res.value}), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    success: function(r){ if(r.d.success) loadGrid(); else Swal.fire("錯誤", r.d.msg, "error"); }
                });
            });
        }
        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function changePageSize() { config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function renderPager(total) {
            let pages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`共 ${total} 筆`);
            let h = "";
            for (let i = 1; i <= pages; i++) {
                h += `<button class="btn btn-sm ${i==config.pageIndex?'btn-primary':'btn-outline-primary'}" onclick="config.pageIndex=${i};loadGrid();">${i}</button>`;
            }
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

了解！您的觀察非常正確。在 SQL Server 中，如果搜尋要達到「模糊比對（包含）」的效果，必須在關鍵字的前後都加上百分比符號 `%`。 如果您目前的搜尋只能抓到「開頭一樣」的資料，代表後端的 SQL 指令可能寫成了 `LIKE @key + '%'`（只有後面有百分比）。 要解決這個問題，我們需要調整 後端 `messagewordvido.aspx.vb` 的參數處理方式。 ### 1. 後端修正：`messagewordvido.aspx.vb` 請檢查您的 `GetData` 函數中，關於 `SqlCommand` 參數的部分。要確保傳給資料庫的字串是 前後都有 `%`。 請將該段落修改為：
vbnet
' 在 GetData 函數內
Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
    conn.Open()
    
    ' 1. 計算總筆數的 SQL (確保前後都有 %)
    Dim countSql As String = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM guestinfovedio WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)"
    Dim countCmd As New SqlCommand(countSql, conn)
    
    ' 關鍵：在這裡前後都加上 %
    Dim searchParam As String = "%" & searchKey & "%"
    countCmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@key", searchParam)
    
    totalCount = CInt(countCmd.ExecuteScalar())

    ' 2. 抓取分頁資料的 SQL
    Dim startRow As Integer = (pageIndex - 1) * pageSize
    ' 注意：這裡的子查詢也要包含模糊搜尋
    Dim sql As String = String.Format( _
        "SELECT TOP {2} 識別碼, name1, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 " & _
        "FROM guestinfovedio WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) AND 識別碼 NOT IN " & _
        "(SELECT TOP {3} 識別碼 FROM guestinfovedio WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}) " & _
        "ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir, pageSize, startRow)

    Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@key", searchParam) ' 同樣使用前後都有 % 的參數
    
    ' ...其餘 da.Fill(dt) 邏輯不變

--- ### 2. 前端檢查：`message5.html` 如果您的搜尋是透過前端 JavaScript 進行「即時過濾」（不回傳資料庫，直接隱藏表格列），請確保使用的是 `.includes()` 或 `indexOf() > -1`。 請檢查 `doSearch` 或相關過濾函數：
javascript
// 如果您是用 JavaScript 直接在網頁上過濾（非資料庫搜尋）
function filterSongs() {
    var input = document.getElementById('inputSearch').value.toLowerCase();
    var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#dataTableBody tr');

    rows.forEach(row => {
        var text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
        // 使用 includes 代表「包含」，不論在前面還是中間都會出現
        if (text.includes(input)) {
            row.style.display = "";
        } else {
            row.style.display = "none";
        }
    });
}
--- ### 為什麼會這樣？ * `LIKE '高%'`：代表搜尋「高」開頭的資料（如：高勝美、高山）。 * `LIKE '%高'`：代表搜尋「高」結尾的資料（如：山很高）。 * `LIKE '%高%'`：代表搜尋「只要有高」就好（如：蔡秋鳳-高山情）。這才是您需要的。 ### 💡 額外建議： 如果您搜尋時輸入的是中文，建議在 SQL 參數中確保使用 `N`（Unicode），在 VB.NET 的 `AddWithValue` 通常會自動處理，但如果您的資料庫編碼較特殊，可以改寫為：
vbnet
' 強制指定為 NVarChar，避免中文字搜尋失效
cmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = "%" & searchKey & "%"
請優先修改 `aspx.vb` 裡面的那個 `@key` 賦值邏輯，搜尋功能就會立刻變強大許多！

🤖 GEMINI
這是在您的原始程式碼基礎上，優化了模糊搜尋（前後百分比 %）以及中文字元處理（NVarChar）後的完整版本。 我同時加入了 `.Trim()` 來防止因為輸入框帶有空白導致搜尋失效的情況：
vbnet
<WebMethod()>
Public Shared Function GetData(ByVal searchKey As String, ByVal sortBy As String, ByVal sortDir As String, ByVal pageIndex As Integer, ByVal pageSize As Integer) As Object
    Try
        Dim rows As New List(Of Dictionary(Of String, Object))()
        Dim totalCount As Integer = 0
        Dim dbCol As String = "識別碼"
        
        ' 1. 對應排序欄位名稱
        Select Case sortBy
            Case "姓名" : dbCol = "name1"
            Case "郵件" : dbCol = "mail1"
            Case "內容" : dbCol = "word1"
            Case "日期" : dbCol = "時間戳記"
            Case Else : dbCol = "識別碼"
        End Select

        ' 2. 處理模糊搜尋字串 (前後加上 %，並去除前後空白)
        Dim finalKey As String = "%" & (searchKey & "").Trim() & "%"

        Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
            conn.Open()

            ' 3. 計算總筆數的 SQL (確保條件與資料抓取一致)
            Dim countSql As String = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)"
            Dim countCmd As New SqlCommand(countSql, conn)
            ' 使用 Add 指定 SqlDbType.NVarChar 可確保中文字搜尋正確
            countCmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
            totalCount = CInt(countCmd.ExecuteScalar())

            ' 4. 分頁資料 SQL 語法
            Dim startRow As Integer = (pageIndex - 1) * pageSize
            Dim sql As String = String.Format(
                "SELECT TOP {2} 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 " &
                "FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) AND 識別碼 NOT IN " &
                "(SELECT TOP {3} 識別碼 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}) " &
                "ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir, pageSize, startRow)

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            Dim dt As New DataTable()
            da.Fill(dt)

            ' 5. 轉換為 Dictionary 格式回傳
            For Each dr As DataRow In dt.Rows
                Dim row As New Dictionary(Of String, Object)()
                For Each col As DataColumn In dt.Columns
                    row.Add(col.ColumnName, dr(col).ToString())
                Next
                rows.Add(row)
            Next
        End Using

        Return New With {.data = rows, .totalCount = totalCount}
    Catch ex As Exception
        Return New With {.error = ex.Message}
    End Try
End Function
### 修改重點： 1. 關鍵修正：將原本的 `AddWithValue` 改為 `countCmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey`。這能強制讓 SQL 以 Unicode 格式比對，解決某些環境下中文字包含搜尋（`%關鍵字%`）失效的問題。 2. 安全性與穩定性： * 加入 `(searchKey & "").Trim()`：避免搜尋關鍵字為空或只有空白時產生錯誤。 * `Case Else`：確保如果 `sortBy` 傳入異常值，系統仍有預設的排序欄位，不會導致 SQL 崩潰。 3. 邏輯一致：確保 `countSql` 與分頁 `sql` 的 `WHERE` 條件完全相同，這樣分頁數字才會準確。

🤖 GEMINI
這是一個非常棒的想法！在電腦（PC/Server）端架設 Web 伺服器，利用電腦強大的 CPU 處理效能來跑 ASP.NET，同時讀取 DS215 裡的檔案。這樣你確實會擁有兩套並行的系統： 1. NAS 版：穩定、省電，適合手機隨時觀看。 2. PC 版：反應極快，適合在電腦端管理大數據量。 要在 Windows PC 端的 IIS (Internet Information Services) 建立指向 NAS 的虛擬目錄，請按照以下步驟操作： --- ### 步驟 1：確保 PC 能自動登入 NAS 檔案路徑 IIS 運作時需要存取權限。首先要確保你的 Windows 已經記住了 NAS 的憑據： 1. 開啟「控制台」 > 「憑據管理員」。 2. 選擇「Windows 憑據」。 3. 新增一項： * 位址：`weizen_ds215` * 帳號/密碼：(輸入你登入 NAS 的帳號密碼)。 * *這樣 IIS 在背景執行時才不會因為沒有權限而讀不到影片。* --- ### 步驟 2：在 IIS 建立虛擬目錄 (vedio1) 1. 按 `Win + R` 輸入 `inetmgr` 開啟 IIS 管理員。 2. 在左側找到你的網站（例如 `Default Web Site`）。 3. 在網站上點 右鍵 > 「新增虛擬目錄...」。 4. 設定如下： * 別名：`vedio1` (務必與你 HTML 程式碼中的名稱一致)。 * 實體路徑：`\\weizen_ds215\video` (直接輸入 NAS 的網路路徑)。 5. 關鍵動作：點擊旁邊的「連接為... (Connect as...)」按鈕。 * 不要選「應用程式使用者」，請選擇「特定使用者」。 * 輸入你 NAS 的帳號密碼（格式建議為：`weizen_ds215\你的帳號`）。 * *這是為了讓 IIS 模擬你的身份去讀取 NAS 檔案。* --- ### 步驟 3：在 IIS 開啟影片串流支援 為了讓 `.mp4` 檔案能順利播放而不變成下載，需要設定 MIME 類型： 1. 在 IIS 中點選剛建好的 `vedio1` 目錄。 2. 在中間找到「MIME 類型」並開啟。 3. 檢查清單中是否有 `.mp4`。如果沒有，請新增： * 副檔名：`.mp4` * MIME 類型：`video/mp4` --- ### 步驟 4：調整你的 HTML 系統 (雙系統切換) 現在你有兩台主機。為了方便切換，你可以在 `message5.html` 中加入一個「切換伺服器」的小邏輯，或者乾脆存成兩份檔案： * PC 版網址：`http://localhost/androidapp/message5.html` (PC 自己看) * PC 版區網網址：`http://[你的電腦IP]/androidapp/message5.html` (其他設備看) 程式碼調整建議： 因為路徑都叫 `vedio1/`，所以你之前的 JavaScript 邏輯完全不需要改！只要把這份 HTML 放到 PC 的網頁目錄（通常是 `C:\inetpub\wwwroot\androidapp\`）下，它就會自動去抓 PC 上的虛擬目錄。 --- ### 💡 這樣做為什麼變快了？ 1. 資料庫查詢快：PC 執行 SQL 運算比 DS215 的處理器快很多。 2. 網頁渲染快：ASP.NET 在 PC 上的反應速度是 NAS 的數倍。 3. 檔案存取不佔 NAS CPU：檔案雖然存在 NAS，但連線管理和網頁協議是由 PC 處理，NAS 只需單純輸出資料流（IO）。 ### ⚠️ 常見問題排除： * 出現 401 或 403 錯誤： 通常是「連接為...」那裡的帳號密碼不對，或者是 NAS 那邊沒有給該帳號足夠的讀取權限。 * 影片無法拖動進度條： 在 IIS 中，請確認「要求篩選」裡沒有封鎖大檔案，並確認 PC 有安裝「HTTP 內容壓縮」或支援「Range Requests」。 你可以先試著建立一個 `vedio1` 的虛擬目錄，然後隨便放一個影片網址測試看看是否能播，成功了我們再把整套資料庫後台搬過去！

Developing the System's Structure I'm currently focused on structuring a message management system, employing HTML/JS with an ASP.NET backend. Key features, including search, sort, pagination, add, edit, and delete, are taking shape. I'm prioritizing usability by implementing a frozen table header for scrolling and dynamic pagination for a cleaner interface. Refining Pagination and Header I've been improving pagination and freezing the table header. The ellipsis logic for dynamic pagination is taking shape, ensuring the first, last, and surrounding pages are always visible. I'm focusing on the CSS to freeze the header, using `position: sticky` and addressing potential clipping issues with `table-responsive`. I'll manage max-heights on the table container to maintain a user-friendly reading experience. Implementing "Show All" I've updated the HTML `<select>` element to include a "Show All" option. In JavaScript, the `changePageSize()` function now interprets "all" as a large number, effectively displaying everything. I'm also reviewing the CSS, noting the use of `max-height` and `overflow-y: auto`. The next step is integrating this into the pagination and the CSS to ensure a consistent experience.
🤖 GEMINI
這針對您提到的三個需求，我已將修改後的代碼整合進原本的架構。 以下是主要的修正重點： 1. 凍結表頭：透過 CSS 的 `position: sticky` 配合 `table-responsive` 的 `max-height` 實現。 2. 智慧分頁：修改 `renderPager` 邏輯，當頁數過多時會顯示省略號 `...`，並確保第一頁與最後一頁永遠可見。 3. 全部顯示：在每頁筆數的下拉選單中加入「全部」選項（邏輯上設為一個極大值）。 ### 修改後的 `message4.html`
html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-Hant">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>專業留言系統 - 優化進階版</title>
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.10.5/font/bootstrap-icons.css">
    <script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.6.0.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>
    <style>
        body {
            background-color: #f0f2f5;
            font-family: "Segoe UI", "Microsoft JhengHei", sans-serif;
        }

        .main-card {
            border-radius: 12px;
            box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
            background: white;
            padding: 25px;
            margin-top: 30px;
        }

        /* 1. 凍結表頭與捲軸設定 */
        .table-container {
            max-height: 65vh; /* 設定表格最大高度 */
            overflow-y: auto;  /* 開啟垂直捲軸 */
            border: 1px solid #dee2e6;
            border-radius: 8px;
        }

        .table thead th {
            position: sticky;
            top: 0;
            z-index: 10;
            background-color: #212529 !important; /* 確保表頭背景不透明 */
            color: white;
            box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 #dee2e6; /* 補齊邊框線 */
        }

        .sort-header {
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: 0.2s;
        }
        .sort-header:hover {
            color: #0d6efd !important;
            background-color: #f8f9fa;
        }

        .content-cell {
            min-width: 300px;
            word-break: break-all;
            white-space: normal;
            line-height: 1.6;
        }

        .nowrap-cell {
            white-space: nowrap;
        }
        
        /* 分頁按鈕美化 */
        .pagination .page-link {
            cursor: pointer;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
  
    <div class="container">
        <div class="main-card">
            <!-- 頂部區 -->
            <div class="row align-items-center mb-4">
                <div class="col-md-6"><h3 class="fw-bold m-0 text-primary"><i class="bi bi-cloud-check"></i> 留言管理系統</h3></div>
                <div class="col-md-6 text-end">
                    <div class="input-group">
                        <input type="text" id="inputSearch" class="form-control" placeholder="關鍵字搜尋...">
                        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="doSearch()">搜尋</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-success" type="button" onclick="openAddModal()">新增留言</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 2. 資料表格容器 (加入凍結表頭容器) -->
            <div class="table-container">
                <table class="table table-hover align-middle m-0">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th onclick="setSort('識別碼')" class="sort-header">ID ⇅</th>
                            <th onclick="setSort('姓名')" class="sort-header">姓名 ⇅</th>
                            <th onclick="setSort('郵件')" class="sort-header">郵件 ⇅</th>
                            <th onclick="setSort('內容')" class="sort-header">內容 ⇅</th>
                            <th onclick="setSort('日期')" class="sort-header">日期 ⇅</th>
                            <th class="text-center">功能</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="dataTableBody">
                        <tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-5">資料載入中...</td></tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <!-- 分頁區 -->
            <div class="row align-items-center mt-4">
                <div class="col-md-4 d-flex align-items-center gap-2">
                    <select id="pageSizeSelect" class="form-select form-select-sm" style="width: 100px;" onchange="changePageSize()">
                        <option value="5">5 筆</option>
                        <option value="10" selected>10 筆</option>
                        <option value="25">25 筆</option>
                        <!-- 3. 全部顯示選項 -->
                        <option value="1000000">全部顯示</option>
                    </select>
                    <span class="small text-muted">每頁</span>
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-4 text-center"><div id="infoLabel" class="small fw-bold text-muted"></div></div>
                <div class="col-md-4">
                    <nav>
                        <ul class="pagination pagination-sm justify-content-end mb-0" id="pagerList">
                            <!-- 分頁會由 JS 動態生成 -->
                        </ul>
                    </nav>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modal 視窗部分保持不變 (新增/編輯) -->
    <!-- ... (這裡省略以節省篇幅，內容與您原本的 message4.html 一致) ... -->

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/message4.aspx";
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10 };
        var addModal, editModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            addModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('addModal'));
            editModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('editModal'));
            loadGrid();
        });

        function callAPI(method, data, successCallback) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(data),
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                crossDomain: true,
                success: function (res) { successCallback(res.d); },
                error: function (xhr) {
                    Swal.fire("API 連線失敗", "請確認伺服器狀態。", "error");
                }
            });
        }

        function loadGrid() {
            callAPI("GetData", config, function (r) {
                if (r.error) { Swal.fire("SQL 錯誤", r.error, "error"); return; }
                renderTable(r.data);
                renderPager(r.totalCount);
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            var html = "";
            if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                html = "<tr><td colspan='6' class='text-center py-5 text-muted'>查無資料</td></tr>";
            } else {
                list.forEach(function (row) {
                    html += `<tr>
                        <td class="nowrap-cell"><span class="badge bg-secondary">${row.識別碼}</span></td>
                        <td class="fw-bold text-primary nowrap-cell">${row.姓名}</td>
                        <td class="small">${row.郵件}</td>
                        <td class="content-cell">${row.內容}</td>
                        <td class="text-muted small nowrap-cell">${row.日期}</td>
                        <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick="openEditModal(${row.識別碼},'${row.姓名}','${row.郵件}','${row.內容}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></button>
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                });
            }
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html);
        }

        // --- 核心改進：智慧分頁邏輯 ---
        function renderPager(total) {
            var totalPages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`第 ${config.pageIndex} / ${totalPages || 1} 頁 (共 ${total} 筆)`);
            
            var h = "";
            var current = config.pageIndex;
            var range = 2; // 當前頁碼前後顯示的數量

            // 上一頁
            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current - 1})">«</a></li>`;

            for (var i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
                // 條件：第一頁、最後一頁、當前頁碼前後 range 範圍內的頁碼
                if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || (i >= current - range && i <= current + range)) {
                    var active = (i === current) ? "active" : "";
                    h += `<li class="page-item ${active}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${i})">${i}</a></li>`;
                } 
                // 顯示省略號的情況
                else if (i === current - range - 1 || i === current + range + 1) {
                    h += `<li class="page-item disabled"><span class="page-link">...</span></li>`;
                }
            }

            // 下一頁
            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === totalPages || totalPages === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current + 1})">»</a></li>`;
            
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }

        function changePage(p) { 
            if (p < 1) return;
            config.pageIndex = p; 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        function changePageSize() { 
            config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); 
            config.pageIndex = 1; 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function setSort(col) { config.sortBy = col; config.sortDir = (config.sortDir === "DESC" ? "ASC" : "DESC"); loadGrid(); }
        
        // Modal & CRUD 操作保持不變...
        // ... (submitAdd, submitUpdate, askDelete 等函數) ...
        function openAddModal() { addModal.show(); }
        function submitAdd() {
            var d = { name: $("#addName").val(), mail: $("#addMail").val(), content: $("#addContent").val() };
            callAPI("InsertData", d, function (r) {
                if (r.success) { Swal.fire("成功", "已新增", "success").then(() => { addModal.hide(); loadGrid(); }); }
            });
        }
        function openEditModal(id, name, mail, content) {
            $("#editId").val(id); $("#editName").val(name); $("#editMail").val(mail); $("#editContent").val(content);
            editModal.show();
        }
        function submitUpdate() {
            var d = { id: $("#editId").val(), name: $("#editName").val(), mail: $("#editMail").val(), content: $("#editContent").val() };
            callAPI("UpdateData", d, function (r) {
                if (r.success) { Swal.fire("成功", "已修改", "success").then(() => { editModal.hide(); loadGrid(); }); }
            });
        }
        function askDelete(id) {
            Swal.fire({ title: '確認刪除？', input: 'password', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) {
                    callAPI("DeleteData", { id: id, pwd: res.value }, function (r) {
                        if (r.success) { Swal.fire("已刪除", "", "success").then(loadGrid); }
                        else { Swal.fire("錯誤", r.msg, "error"); }
                    });
                }
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
### 優化點詳細說明： 1. 凍結表頭 (Sticky Header): * 我新增了一個 `.table-container` 類別，並設定了 `max-height: 65vh` (螢幕高度的 65%)。 * 在 CSS 中針對 `thead th` 使用 `position: sticky; top: 0;`。這讓您在向下捲動瀏覽上百條留言時，黑色標題列會固定在最上方，方便對照欄位。 2. 智慧型分頁 (Smart Pagination): * 修改了 `renderPager`。現在如果總頁數非常多（例如 100 頁），它不會全部列出。 * 它會顯示：`1 ... 4 5 (6) 7 8 ... 100`。 * 這樣可以確保分頁按鈕永遠不會撐破頁面佈局，且使用者依然可以快速跳轉到第一頁或最後一頁。 3. 全部顯示 (Show All): * 在 `pageSizeSelect` 下拉選單加入 `<option value="1000000">全部顯示</option>`。 * 當選擇此項時，`pageSize` 會變成一百萬，後端的 `GetData` 就會一次把所有資料抓回來（除非您的資料量超過百萬筆）。 4. 分頁資訊增強: * 在分頁狀態列加入了 `(共 X 筆)` 的字樣，讓管理者清楚目前資料庫的總量。

I've pinpointed that data becomes visible when pagination or page size options trigger `loadGrid()`. It appears the initial call within `$(document).ready()` isn't functioning as expected, and I'm looking into how to resolve this. Debugging Initial Load Failure I've been going deeper. I'm focused on why the table isn't rendering on the initial page load, even with `$(document).ready()` present. I'm exploring server-side expectations, and asynchronous modal initialization as potential causes, moving past client-side DOM or config initializations. Hypothesis 5, specific server-side header issues, is the current focus, and I'm investigating potential discrepancies between the initial request and subsequent ones triggered by pagination. I have started working on Hypothesis 6 as a side investigation. Refining API Call Synchronization I'm now honing in on potential API synchronization issues. It seems the initial data fetch might be hindered by mismatched parameter names or values between the client and server. I'm exploring if the `config.pageSize` from the HTML `<select>` needs explicit syncing before the first call, and I'll carefully examine the `callAPI` function to ensure data structure consistency between the initial request and later calls. Additionally, I'll explore more robust ways to trigger the first `loadGrid()` call. Investigating Initial Rendering I'm now focusing on the immediate rendering failure. I've considered a timeout for the initial call, but I'm moving toward examining `renderPager` and `pageSizeSelect`. If the data arrives, why isn't it displaying? Perhaps the dropdown's value is causing issues, but it's hardcoded. The key is to match `config.pageSize` with the HTML's selection. I'll add a loading indicator, and check the parameters sent. Also, there may have been a row mapping issue. Addressing Initial Load Issues I'm now zeroing in on the rendering of the pager. If the initial `total` is undefined, `totalPages` will be 0, but that doesn't explain the missing table. I'm focusing on strengthening the initialization process. I'm changing `$(document).ready` to sync the UI with the config, ensuring that `config.pageSize` reflects the selected value, and then calling `loadGrid`. I'll also add debugging logs. I'll test the "Show All" option, and the sticky positioning.
🤖 GEMINI
這通常是因為 初始化時 `config` 物件的數值與 HTML 選單沒有同步，或是 JavaScript 執行的順序 導致第一次呼叫時參數未正確帶入。 我優化了 `$(document).ready` 的邏輯，確保在第一次啟動時，先從 HTML 的選單抓取預設值（例如 10 筆），再執行 `loadGrid()`。同時我加入了一些防錯機制。 以下是修正後的完整 `script` 部分與 HTML 結構： ### 修正後的 `message4.html` (重點在 Script 初始化)

    <!-- Modal 部分 (新增/編輯) 保持不變，略... -->
    <!-- 請確保您的代碼中有 id="addModal" 和 id="editModal" 的視窗 -->

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/message4.aspx";
        
        // 初始設定
        var config = { 
            searchKey: "", 
            sortBy: "識別碼", 
            sortDir: "DESC", 
            pageIndex: 1, 
            pageSize: 10 
        };

        var addModal, editModal;

        // --- 修正啟動邏輯 ---
        $(document).ready(function () {
            // 1. 初始化 Bootstrap Modal
            var addEl = document.getElementById('addModal');
            var editEl = document.getElementById('editModal');
            if(addEl) addModal = new bootstrap.Modal(addEl);
            if(editEl) editModal = new bootstrap.Modal(editEl);

            // 2. 關鍵修正：確保 config 的 pageSize 與 HTML Select 預設值同步
            config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()) || 10;

            // 3. 執行第一次載入
            loadGrid();
        });

        function loadGrid() {
            // 執行請求前，清空目前表格顯示 loading
            // $("#dataTableBody").html('<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-5">載入中...</td></tr>');

            callAPI("GetData", config, function (r) {
                if (r.error) { 
                    Swal.fire("SQL 錯誤", r.error, "error"); 
                    $("#dataTableBody").html('<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-5 text-danger">載入失敗</td></tr>');
                    return; 
                }
                
                // 檢查是否有回傳資料
                if (r.data) {
                    renderTable(r.data);
                    renderPager(r.totalCount);
                } else {
                    $("#dataTableBody").html('<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-5">無資料</td></tr>');
                }
            });
        }

        function callAPI(method, data, successCallback) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(data),
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                crossDomain: true,
                success: function (res) { 
                    // ASP.NET WebMethod 會將結果封裝在 .d 裡面
                    successCallback(res.d); 
                },
                error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error("API Error:", status, error);
                    Swal.fire("連線失敗", "無法從伺服器取得資料，請檢查網路或 CORS 設定。", "error");
                }
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            var html = "";
            if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                html = "<tr><td colspan='6' class='text-center py-5 text-muted'>查無資料</td></tr>";
            } else {
                list.forEach(function (row) {
                    html += `<tr>
                        <td class="nowrap-cell"><span class="badge bg-secondary">${row.識別碼 || row.ID}</span></td>
                        <td class="fw-bold text-primary nowrap-cell">${row.姓名 || row.Name}</td>
                        <td class="small">${row.郵件 || row.Email}</td>
                        <td class="content-cell">${row.內容 || row.Content}</td>
                        <td class="text-muted small nowrap-cell">${row.日期 || row.Date}</td>
                        <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick="openEditModal('${row.識別碼}','${row.姓名}','${row.郵件}','${row.內容}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></button>
                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                });
            }
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html);
        }

        function renderPager(total) {
            var totalPages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`第 ${config.pageIndex} / ${totalPages || 1} 頁 (共 ${total} 筆)`);
            
            var h = "";
            var current = config.pageIndex;
            var range = 2; 

            // 上一頁
            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current - 1})">«</a></li>`;

            for (var i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
                if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || (i >= current - range && i <= current + range)) {
                    var active = (i === current) ? "active" : "";
                    h += `<li class="page-item ${active}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${i})">${i}</a></li>`;
                } 
                else if (i === current - range - 1 || i === current + range + 1) {
                    h += `<li class="page-item disabled"><span class="page-link">...</span></li>`;
                }
            }

            // 下一頁
            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === totalPages || totalPages === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current + 1})">»</a></li>`;
            
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }

        // --- 事件處理 ---
        function changePage(p) { 
            var totalPages = Math.ceil(parseInt($("#infoLabel").text().split('共 ')[1]) / config.pageSize);
            if (p < 1 || (totalPages > 0 && p > totalPages)) return;
            config.pageIndex = p; 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        function changePageSize() { 
            config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); 
            config.pageIndex = 1; 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        function doSearch() { 
            config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); 
            config.pageIndex = 1; 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        function setSort(col) { 
            config.sortBy = col; 
            config.sortDir = (config.sortDir === "DESC" ? "ASC" : "DESC"); 
            loadGrid(); 
        }

        // 其餘新增、刪除函數...
    </script>
</body>
</html>
### 為什麼這能修復「第一次載入無資料」？ 1. 顯式同步 `pageSize`：在 `$(document).ready` 中，我強制執行了 `config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val())`。這樣可以避免 `config` 物件裡的預設值與 HTML 標籤的值不一致，導致伺服器端接收到錯誤的分頁參數。 2. AJAX 錯誤追蹤：我在 `callAPI` 中加入了 `console.error`。如果第一次載入還是沒資料，您可以按 F12 查看「Console」面板，看看是否出現了 `CORS` 或 `404` 錯誤。 3. 後端回傳結構相容：我在 `renderTable` 中使用了 `row.識別碼 || row.ID` 這種寫法。有時候伺服器端第一次回傳的欄位名稱大小寫與後續不同（雖然罕見），這能增加相容性。 4. Loading 提示：在 HTML 中 `<tbody>` 預設放了一行「載入中」，如果請求成功但資料為空，會被「查無資料」取代；如果沒反應，畫面上會一直停在「載入中」，這能幫您判斷是「沒抓到資料」還是「程式完全沒執行」。 請嘗試更新此版本，應該就能在開啟網頁時立刻看到資料了。

        // 統一呼叫 API 函數
        function callAPI(method, data, successCallback) {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(data),
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                dataType: "json",
                crossDomain: true,
                success: function (res) { successCallback(res.d); },
                error: function (xhr) {
                    Swal.fire("API 連線失敗", "伺服器無回應，請檢查後端設定。", "error");
                }
            });
        }

        function loadGrid() {
            callAPI("GetData", config, function (r) {
                if (r.error) { Swal.fire("SQL 錯誤", r.error, "error"); return; }
                renderTable(r.data);
                renderPager(r.totalCount);
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            var html = "";
            if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                html = "<tr><td colspan='6' class='text-center py-5 text-muted'>查無資料</td></tr>";
            } else {
                list.forEach(function (row) {
                    html += `<tr>
                        <td class="nowrap-cell"><span class="badge bg-secondary">${row.識別碼}</span></td>

                        <td class="content-cell">${row.內容}</td>

                            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                });
            }
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html);
        }

        function renderPager(total) {
            var totalPages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`第 ${config.pageIndex} / ${totalPages || 1} 頁 (共 ${total} 筆)`);
            var h = "";
            var current = config.pageIndex;
            var range = 2; 

            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current - 1})">«</a></li>`;
            for (var i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
                if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || (i >= current - range && i <= current + range)) {

                } else if (i === current - range - 1 || i === current + range + 1) {
                    h += `<li class="page-item disabled"><span class="page-link">...</span></li>`;
                }
            }
            h += `<li class="page-item ${current === totalPages || totalPages === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}"><a class="page-link" onclick="changePage(${current + 1})">»</a></li>`;
            $("#pagerList").html(h);

        // --- 功能函數實作 ---
        function openAddModal() { addModal.show(); }
        
        function submitAdd() {
            var d = { name: $("#addName").val(), mail: $("#addMail").val(), content: $("#addContent").val() };
            if (!d.name || !d.content) { Swal.fire("欄位未填", "姓名與內容不能為空", "warning"); return; }
            callAPI("InsertData", d, function (r) {

        function submitUpdate() {

            callAPI("UpdateData", d, function (r) {
                if (r.success) {

                        if (r.success) { Swal.fire("已刪除", "", "success").then(loadGrid); }
                        else { Swal.fire("錯誤", r.msg, "error"); }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        function changePage(p) { if (p < 1) return; config.pageIndex = p; loadGrid(); }

        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function setSort(col) { 
            config.sortBy = col; 
            config.sortDir = (config.sortDir === "DESC" ? "ASC" : "DESC");

👤 USER
搜尋好像未包含郵件欄位

🧠思維過程 (THOUGHT)
Gemini 的思考路徑：
Analyzing Search Scope I've observed that the email field (`mail1`) is currently omitted from the search function within the message system. The frontend, specifically `message4.html`, submits a `searchKey`. My investigation now focuses on how the backend (`message4.aspx.vb`) utilizes this key, and I'm assessing potential integration points for expanding the search scope to include the email field.
🤖 GEMINI
這是因為後端 `message4.aspx.vb` 中的 SQL 指令只比對了「姓名」與「內容」，漏掉了「郵件」欄位。 要修正這個問題，您需要修改後端 VB 程式碼 中的 `GetData` 函數。 ### 修正後的後端 `GetData` 邏輯 (`message4.aspx.vb`) 請將您的 `GetData` 函數中的 SQL 語句修改為如下內容（重點在 `WHERE` 條件）：
vbnet
<WebMethod()>
Public Shared Function GetData(ByVal searchKey As String, ByVal sortBy As String, ByVal sortDir As String, ByVal pageIndex As Integer, ByVal pageSize As Integer) As Object
    Try
        Dim rows As New List(Of Dictionary(Of String, Object))()
        Dim totalCount As Integer = 0
        Dim dbCol As String = "識別碼"
        Select Case sortBy
            Case "姓名" : dbCol = "name1"
            Case "郵件" : dbCol = "mail1"
            Case "內容" : dbCol = "word1"
            Case "日期" : dbCol = "時間戳記"
        End Select

        ' 處理模糊搜尋字串
        Dim finalKey As String = "%" & (searchKey & "").Trim() & "%"

        Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
            conn.Open()

            ' 1. 修改計算總筆數的 SQL (加入 mail1 LIKE @key)
            Dim countSql As String = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)"
            Dim countCmd As New SqlCommand(countSql, conn)
            countCmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
            totalCount = CInt(countCmd.ExecuteScalar())

            ' 2. 修改分頁資料 SQL (加入 mail1 LIKE @key)
            Dim startRow As Integer = (pageIndex - 1) * pageSize
            Dim sql As String = String.Format(
                "SELECT TOP {2} 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 " &
                "FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) AND 識別碼 NOT IN " &
                "(SELECT TOP {3} 識別碼 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}) " &
                "ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir, pageSize, startRow)

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            Dim dt As New DataTable()
            da.Fill(dt)

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt.Rows
                Dim row As New Dictionary(Of String, Object)()
                For Each col As DataColumn In dt.Columns
                    row.Add(col.ColumnName, dr(col).ToString())
                Next
                rows.Add(row)
            Next
        End Using

        Return New With {.data = rows, .totalCount = totalCount}
    Catch ex As Exception
        Return New With {.error = ex.Message}
    End Try
End Function
### 修正重點： 1. `WHERE` 條件增加：在 `countSql` 與分頁 `sql` 中，都將條件改成了 `(name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)`。這樣搜尋時，系統會同時比對姓名、郵件、留言內容。 2. SQL 參數一致性：確保分頁用的子查詢（`SELECT TOP {3}...`）裡的搜尋條件跟主查詢完全一致，否則分頁結果會出現跳號或重複。 ### 前端檢查 (`message4.html`)： 前端通常不需要大改，但請確認您的 `doSearch()` 函數有正確抓到輸入值：
javascript
function doSearch() { 
    config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); // 確保抓到搜尋框的值
    config.pageIndex = 1; // 搜尋時強迫回到第一頁
    loadGrid(); 
}
修改完後端 `.aspx.vb` 檔案後，重新編譯或存檔，搜尋功能就能抓到「郵件」欄位的內容了。

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/mymvc/messagewordvido.aspx";
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10000000 };
        var playQueue = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dbPlayQueue')) || [];
        var dataModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            dataModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('dataModal'));
            // 初始化時讀取下拉選單的值 (預設為全部)
            config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val());
            renderQueue();
            loadGrid();
        });

        function loadGrid() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/GetData",
                data: JSON.stringify(config), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function (res) {
                    renderTable(res.d.data);
                    renderPager(res.d.totalCount);
                }
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            let html = "";
            list.forEach(row => {
                let typeName = "", typeClass = "";
                if (row.郵件 == "1") { typeName = "電腦"; typeClass = "bg-secondary"; }
                else if (row.郵件 == "2") { typeName = "實體"; typeClass = "bg-success"; }
                else if (row.郵件 == "3") { typeName = "虛擬"; typeClass = "bg-info text-dark"; }
                else if (row.郵件 == "4") { typeName = "YT"; typeClass = "bg-danger"; }

                html += `<tr id="tr_${row.識別碼}">
                    <td><span class="small text-muted">${row.識別碼}</span></td>
                    <td><span class="badge ${typeClass} badge-type">${typeName}</span></td>
                    <td class="fw-bold">${row.姓名}</td>
                    <td class="path-text text-truncate">${row.內容}</td>
                    <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-play" onclick="handleSmartPlay('${row.內容}', '${row.姓名}', '${row.郵件}', this)"><i class="bi bi-play-fill"></i></button>
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-queue ms-1" onclick="addToQueue('${row.內容}', '${row.姓名}', '${row.郵件}')">＋</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary ms-2" onclick="openEdit('${row.識別碼}','${row.姓名}','${row.郵件}','${row.內容}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger ms-1" onclick="askDelete(${row.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                    </td>
                </tr>`;
            });
            $("#dataTableBody").html(html || "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center'>查無資料</td></tr>");
        }

        // --- 智慧播放與路徑處理 ---
        function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
            if (type === "4") return rawPath;
            let dp = decodeURIComponent(rawPath);
            let fn = dp.split('/').pop();
            return (type === "2" ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/") + encodeURIComponent(fn);
        }

        function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn) {
            $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
            if (btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");
            if (type === "4") { window.open(rawPath, '_blank'); return; }

            const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
            player.src = getFinalSrc(rawPath, type);
            player.load();
            player.play().catch(() => { $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("⚠️ 播放被阻擋，請手動點擊"); });
            $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("▶ 正在播放: " + title);
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        // --- 點歌與連播 ---
        function addToQueue(src, title, type) {
            playQueue.push({ src, title, type });
            saveAndRenderQueue();
            Swal.fire({ title: '已加入待播', text: title, icon: 'success', toast: true, position: 'top-end', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });
        }

        function playNext() {
            if (playQueue.length > 0) {
                const next = playQueue.shift();
                saveAndRenderQueue();
                handleSmartPlay(next.src, next.title, next.type, null);
            } else {
                let nextBtn = $(".active-row").next("tr").find(".btn-play");
                if (nextBtn.length > 0) nextBtn.click();
                else Swal.fire("播完了", "已到底端", "info");
            }
        }
        document.getElementById('mainPlayer').onended = playNext;

        function saveAndRenderQueue() {
            localStorage.setItem('dbPlayQueue', JSON.stringify(playQueue));
            renderQueue();
        }

        function renderQueue() {
            $("#queueCount").text(playQueue.length);
            let h = playQueue.map((item, idx) => `
                <div class="queue-item"><span>${idx + 1}. ${item.title}</span><i class="bi bi-x-circle text-danger" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="removeFromQueue(${idx})"></i></div>`).join('');
            $("#queueList").html(h || '<div class="p-2 text-muted text-center small">無待播歌曲</div>');
        }

        function removeFromQueue(idx) { playQueue.splice(idx, 1); saveAndRenderQueue(); }
        function toggleQueueUI() { $("#queueUI").toggleClass("active"); }

        // --- 智慧型分頁渲染 ---
        function renderPager(total) {
            let totalPages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
            $("#infoLabel").text(`共 ${total} 筆`);
            if (totalPages <= 1) { $("#pagerList").empty(); return; }

            let h = "";
            let current = config.pageIndex;
            let range = 2; // 當前頁碼前後顯示幾頁

            // 上一頁
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${current==1?'disabled':''} onclick="changePage(${current-1})">«</button>`;

            for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
                if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || (i >= current - range && i <= current + range)) {
                    h += `<button class="btn btn-sm ${i==current?'btn-primary':'btn-outline-primary'}" onclick="changePage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
                } else if (i === current - range - 1 || i === current + range + 1) {
                    h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light" disabled>...</button>`;
                }
            }

            // 下一頁
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${current==totalPages?'disabled':''} onclick="changePage(${current+1})">»</button>`;
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }

        function changePage(p) { config.pageIndex = p; loadGrid(); }
        function changePageSize() { config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function setSort(col) { config.sortBy = col; config.sortDir = (config.sortDir === "DESC" ? "ASC" : "DESC"); loadGrid(); }

        // --- CRUD 操作 (Insert/Update/Delete) ---
        function openAddModal() {
            $("#modalTitle").text("新增影音資料");
            $("#editId").val(""); $("#fieldName").val(""); $("#fieldContent").val(""); $("#fieldMail").val("3");
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('InsertData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function openEdit(id, name, mail, content) {
            $("#modalTitle").text("修改資料 #" + id);
            $("#editId").val(id); $("#fieldName").val(name); $("#fieldMail").val(mail); $("#fieldContent").val(content);
            $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('UpdateData')");
            dataModal.show();
        }
        function submitData(method) {
            let d = { id: $("#editId").val() || 0, name: $("#fieldName").val(), mail: $("#fieldMail").val(), content: $("#fieldContent").val() };
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/" + method,
                data: JSON.stringify(d), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function () { dataModal.hide(); loadGrid(); Swal.fire("成功", "", "success"); }
            });
        }
        function askDelete(id) {
            Swal.fire({ title: '刪除資料？', input: 'password', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) $.ajax({
                    type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/DeleteData",
                    data: JSON.stringify({ id: id, pwd: res.value }), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    success: function (r) { if (r.d.success) loadGrid(); else Swal.fire("錯誤", r.d.msg, "error"); }
                });
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
### 功能說明： 1. 凍結表頭 (Table Scroll)： * 我使用了 `.table-container` 包裹表格，並設定 `max-height: 60vh`。 * CSS 中的 `thead th` 設定了 `sticky`，當您資料非常多（如全部顯示時），滾動頁面時黑色表頭會固定在頂部播放器的下方。 2. 智慧分頁邏輯： * 在 `renderPager` 中，我加入了縮合邏輯。如果總頁數非常多，它不會列出 1 2 3...100，而是會顯示 `1 ... 5 6 (7) 8 9 ... 100`。 * 這可以防止按鈕過多撐破版面。 3. 預設顯示全部： * HTML 中 `<option value="10000000" selected>全部顯示</option>`。 * JS 的 `config` 物件中預設 `pageSize: 10000000`。 * 程式載入時會自動抓取這個大數值，並向資料庫請求所有資料。 4. 穩定性與安全： * `decodeURIComponent` 與 `encodeURIComponent` 的組合使用，確保了含中文、空格與特殊符號的影片檔名在點擊時能被正確轉換成網址，避免 `400 Bad Request`。 您現在可以直接將這份代碼上傳覆蓋原本的檔案，功能應該就完美到位了！

🧠思維過程 (THOUGHT)
Gemini 的思考路徑：
Analyzing the Failure I'm currently investigating a `500 Internal Server Error` on the `/GetData` endpoint. The error surfaced after the "Show All" feature was implemented, alongside sorting and pagination. I'm looking at potential memory exhaustion issues or inefficient database queries stemming from the large `pageSize = 10000000`. I'm going to look at related pages, such as `messagewordvido.aspx` and `message5.html`, to see if there is any overlap in the code. Addressing the Limits I've determined the JSON serialization limit is the prime suspect for "Show All" 500 errors. I'll increase `maxJsonLength` in `web.config` to tackle this. Additionally, I'll verify the `GetData` method signature precisely matches the JavaScript's parameter names and types to avoid parameter mismatch errors. Then, I will address the SQL query. Refining the SQL Okay, the focus now is on optimizing the SQL query itself, particularly for the "Show All" scenario. I'm going to implement a specific check in the VB.NET code. If the `pageSize` is excessively large, I will bypass the complex `NOT IN` subquery in favor of a simpler, more direct `SELECT` statement. This should prevent potential performance bottlenecks. I am adding robust error logging to pinpoint any issues, and double-checking that parameters from the AJAX call perfectly align with the `GetData` method's signature.
🤖 GEMINI
出現 `500 Internal Server Error` 代表伺服器後端程式碼在執行時崩潰了。根據您剛才增加的「全部顯示」功能（`pageSize` 設為一千萬），最可能的導火線有兩個： 1. JSON 長度限制：當選取「全部顯示」時，回傳的資料量太大，超過了 ASP.NET 預設的 JSON 序列化長度限制。 2. SQL 語法錯誤：當 `pageSize` 非常大時，原本分頁用的 `SELECT TOP ... NOT IN` 邏輯可能在處理邊界值時出錯。 請按照以下步驟修正： ### 修正一：修改 `Web.config` (解決 500 錯誤的核心) 這是最常見的原因。ASP.NET 預設只允許很短的 JSON 回傳，請在您的專案 `Web.config` 檔案中加入以下設定，放大傳輸限制：
xml
<configuration>
  <system.web.extensions>
    <scripting>
      <webServices>
        <!-- 設定為 2GB，確保大量資料時不會報 500 錯誤 -->
        <jsonSerialization maxJsonLength="2147483647" />
      </webServices>
    </scripting>
  </system.web.extensions>
</configuration>
--- ### 修正二：優化後端 `GetData` 邏輯 (`aspx.vb`) 當 `pageSize` 很大（全部顯示）時，我們不應該跑複雜的分頁子查詢，改用簡單的查詢以提高效能並避免語法錯誤。 請更新您的 `GetData` 函數：
vbnet
<WebMethod()>
Public Shared Function GetData(ByVal searchKey As String, ByVal sortBy As String, ByVal sortDir As String, ByVal pageIndex As Integer, ByVal pageSize As Integer) As Object
    Try
        Dim rows As New List(Of Dictionary(Of String, Object))()
        Dim totalCount As Integer = 0
        Dim dbCol As String = "識別碼"
        
        Select Case sortBy
            Case "姓名" : dbCol = "name1"
            Case "郵件" : dbCol = "mail1"
            Case "內容" : dbCol = "word1"
            Case "日期" : dbCol = "時間戳記"
        End Select

        Dim finalKey As String = "%" & (searchKey & "").Trim() & "%"

        Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
            conn.Open()

            ' 1. 計算總數
            Dim countSql As String = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)"
            Dim countCmd As New SqlCommand(countSql, conn)
            countCmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
            totalCount = CInt(countCmd.ExecuteScalar())

            ' 2. 根據 pageSize 決定 SQL 策略
            Dim sql As String = ""
            If pageSize >= 1000000 Then
                ' 如果是「全部顯示」，使用簡單查詢
                sql = String.Format("SELECT 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 " & _
                                  "FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) " & _
                                  "ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir)
            else
                ' 如果是正常分頁，使用 TOP NOT IN 邏輯
                Dim startRow As Integer = (pageIndex - 1) * pageSize
                sql = String.Format(
                    "SELECT TOP {2} 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 " &
                    "FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) AND 識別碼 NOT IN " &
                    "(SELECT TOP {3} 識別碼 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}) " &
                    "ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir, pageSize, startRow)
            End If

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
            
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            Dim dt As New DataTable()
            da.Fill(dt)

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt.Rows
                Dim row As New Dictionary(Of String, Object)()
                For Each col As DataColumn In dt.Columns
                    row.Add(col.ColumnName, dr(col).ToString())
                Next
                rows.Add(row)
            Next
        End Using

        Return New With {.data = rows, .totalCount = totalCount}
    Catch ex As Exception
        ' 這裡發生錯誤時會回傳給前端 Swal 顯示
        Return New With {.error = ex.Message}
    End Try
End Function
--- ### 修正三：前端分頁 UI 防呆 (`message5.html`) 如果資料只有一頁，我們讓分頁按鈕乾脆不要出現，避免畫面混亂。請更新 `renderPager`：
javascript
function renderPager(total) {
    let totalPages = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize);
    $("#infoLabel").text(`(共 ${total} 筆)`);
    
    // 如果只有一頁或選擇全部顯示，清空分頁按鈕並跳出
    if (totalPages <= 1 || config.pageSize >= 1000000) { 
        $("#pagerList").empty(); 
        return; 
    }

    let h = "";
    let current = config.pageIndex;
    let range = 2;

    // 上一頁按鈕
    h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${current==1?'disabled':''} onclick="changePage(${current-1})">«</button>`;

    for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
        if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || (i >= current - range && i <= current + range)) {
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm ${i==current?'btn-primary':'btn-outline-primary'}" onclick="changePage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
        } else if (i === current - range - 1 || i === current + range + 1) {
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light" disabled>...</button>`;
        }
    }

    // 下一頁按鈕
    h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${current==totalPages?'disabled':''} onclick="changePage(${current+1})">»</button>`;
    $("#pagerList").html(h);
}
### 總結排除步驟： 1. 優先修改 `Web.config`：這通常能直接解決 500 錯誤。 2. 更新 `aspx.vb`：這能解決當資料量過大時分頁邏輯崩潰的問題。 3. 確認 `target_folder`：如果您在 File Station 看到的資料夾是 `vedio1`，請確保 `handleSmartPlay` 裡的取代路徑一致。 如果執行後還有 500 錯誤，請查看 Windows 事件檢視器 或 IIS 日誌，那裡會顯示具體的 VB.NET 錯誤行號。

根據您的要求，我重新整理了代碼。本次修正重點如下： 1. 移除凍結表頭：表格恢復為自然長度，隨網頁捲動。 2. 智慧型分頁（Smart Pager）：當頁數過多時，會自動顯示為 `1 ... 5 6 (7) 8 9 ... 100` 格式，防止按鈕撐破版面。 3. 預設顯示全部：將 `pageSize` 預設為 `10000000`，且下拉選單初始選中「全部」。 4. 保留點歌與播放功能：包含 `+` 號點歌、下一首、以及路徑自動轉換邏輯。 ### 修正版 `message5.html`

<body>

    <!-- [1. 置頂播放器與控制區] -->
    <div class="player-sticky-zone">
        <video id="mainPlayer" controls playsinline></video>
        <div class="control-strip">
            <div id="nowPlayingTitle">💡 請選擇影片或按 + 號加入待播</div>
            <div class="d-flex gap-2 justify-content-center">
                <input type="text" id="inputSearch" class="form-control form-control-sm" style="max-width: 250px;" placeholder="搜尋..." onkeyup="if(event.keyCode==13) doSearch()">
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="doSearch()">搜尋</button>
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="playNext()">下一首</button>

        <div class="queue-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleQueueUI()">📍 待播清單 (<span id="queueCount">0</span>) - 展開/收合</div>

    <div class="container pb-5">

            <!-- [分頁控制] -->
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mt-4">
                <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2">
                    <select id="pageSizeSelect" class="form-select form-select-sm" style="width: 105px;" onchange="changePageSize()">
                        <option value="5">5 筆</option>
                        <option value="10">10 筆</option>
                        <option value="25">25 筆</option>
                        <option value="100">100 筆</option>
                        <option value="10000000" selected>全部顯示</option>
                    </select>

    <!-- [Modal] 新增/編輯視窗 -->
    <div class="modal fade" id="dataModal" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="modal-dialog">
            <div class="modal-content">
                <div class="modal-header"><h5 class="modal-title" id="modalTitle">資料維護</h5><button class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal"></button></div>
                <div class="modal-body">
                    <input type="hidden" id="editId">
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label class="form-label fw-bold">來源類型 (mail1)</label>
                        <select id="fieldMail" class="form-select">
                            <option value="1">1. 電腦類 (vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="2">2. NAS 實體 (vedio)</option>
                            <option value="3">3. NAS 虛擬 (vedio1)</option>
                            <option value="4">4. YouTube 連結</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3"><label class="form-label">路徑或網址 (word1)</label><textarea id="fieldContent" class="form-control" rows="3"></textarea></div>
