    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "message5.aspx"; // 同目錄下
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10000000 };
        var playQueue = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dbPlayQueue')) || [];
        var dataModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            dataModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('dataModal'));
            config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val());
            loadSlots();
            renderQueue();
            loadGrid();
        });

        // --- 存檔位系統 ---
        function loadSlots() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/GetSlotNames", data: "{}", contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function(res) {
                    let h = '<option value="">-- 載入存檔位 --</option>';
                    res.d.forEach(s => { h += `<option value="${s.id}">${s.id}. ${s.name}</option>`; });
                    $("#slotSelector").html(h);
                }
            });
        }

        $("#slotSelector").on("change", function() {
            let sid = $(this).val(); if(!sid) return;
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/LoadPlaylistItems", data: JSON.stringify({ slotId: sid }), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function(res) {
                    if(res.d.data) {
                        playQueue = res.d.data.map(i => ({ id: i.識別碼, src: i.內容, title: i.姓名, type: i.郵件 }));
                        saveAndRenderQueue();
                        Swal.fire("載入成功", "待播清單已更新", "success");
                    }
                }
            });
        });

        function saveToSlot() {
            let sid = $("#slotSelector").val();
            if(!sid) { Swal.fire("請先選一個編號", "", "warning"); return; }
            Swal.fire({ title: '命名此清單', input: 'text', inputValue: $("#slotSelector option:selected").text().split('. ')[1], showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) {
                    let idList = playQueue.map(q => q.id).join(',');
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/SavePlaylist",
                        data: JSON.stringify({ slotId: sid, slotName: res.value, ids: idList }), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                        success: function() { Swal.fire("存檔成功", "", "success"); loadSlots(); }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // --- 播放核心邏輯 ---
        function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
            if (type == "4") return rawPath;
            let dp = decodeURIComponent(rawPath);
            let fn = dp.split('/').pop();
            let folder = (type == "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";
            return folder + encodeURIComponent(fn);
        }

        function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn, id) {
            $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
            if (btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");
            if (type == "4") { window.open(rawPath, '_blank'); return; }

            const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
            const finalURL = getFinalSrc(rawPath, type);
            player.src = finalURL;
            player.load();
            player.play().then(() => {
                $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("▶ 正在播放: " + title);
            }).catch(() => {
                $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("⚠️ 點擊播放鈕啟動：" + title);
            });
        }

        function playNext() {
            if (playQueue.length > 0) {
                const next = playQueue.shift();
                saveAndRenderQueue();
                handleSmartPlay(next.src, next.title, next.type, null, next.id);
            } else {
                let nextRow = $(".active-row").next("tr");
                if (!nextRow.length) nextRow = $("#dataTableBody tr:first");
                if (nextRow.length) {
                    nextRow.find(".btn-play").click();
                    nextRow[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                }
            }
        }
        document.getElementById('mainPlayer').onended = playNext;

        // --- 管理與表格功能 ---
        function loadGrid() {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/GetData", data: JSON.stringify(config), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                success: function (res) { renderTable(res.d.data); renderPager(res.d.totalCount); }
            });
        }

        function renderTable(list) {
            let h = "";
            list.forEach(item => {
                let tName = item.郵件=="1"?"電腦":item.郵件=="2"?"實體":item.郵件=="3"?"虛擬":"YT";
                let tClass = item.郵件=="1"?"bg-secondary":item.郵件=="2"?"bg-success":item.郵件=="3"?"bg-info text-dark":"bg-danger";
                h += `<tr>
                    <td><span class="small text-muted">${item.識別碼}</span></td>
                    <td><span class="badge ${tClass}">${tName}</span></td>
                    <td class="fw-bold">${item.姓名}</td>
                    <td class="path-text text-truncate">${item.內容}</td>
                    <td class="text-center nowrap-cell">
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-play" onclick="handleSmartPlay('${item.內容}', '${item.姓名}', '${item.郵件}', this, ${item.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-play-fill"></i></button>
                        <button class="btn-action-circle btn-queue ms-1" onclick="addToQueue(${item.識別碼}, '${item.內容}', '${item.姓名}', '${item.郵件}')">＋</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary ms-2" onclick="openEdit('${item.識別碼}','${item.姓名}','${item.郵件}','${item.內容}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></button>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger ms-1" onclick="askDelete(${item.識別碼})"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>
                    </td>
                </tr>`;
            });
            $("#dataTableBody").html(h || "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center'>查無資料</td></tr>");
        }

        function renderPager(total) {
            let tp = Math.ceil(total / config.pageSize); $("#infoLabel").text(`(共 ${total} 筆)`);
            if (tp <= 1 || config.pageSize >= 1000000) { $("#pagerList").empty(); return; }
            let h = "", cur = config.pageIndex, ran = 2;
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${cur==1?'disabled':''} onclick="config.pageIndex=${cur-1};loadGrid()">«</button>`;
            for (let i = 1; i <= tp; i++) {
                if (i == 1 || i == tp || (i >= cur - ran && i <= cur + ran)) h += `<button class="btn btn-sm ${i==cur?'btn-primary':'btn-outline-primary'}" onclick="config.pageIndex=${i};loadGrid()">${i}</button>`;
                else if (i == cur - ran - 1 || i == cur + ran + 1) h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light" disabled>...</button>`;
            }
            h += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" ${cur==tp?'disabled':''} onclick="config.pageIndex=${cur+1};loadGrid()">»</button>`;
            $("#pagerList").html(h);
        }

        function addToQueue(id, src, title, type) {
            playQueue.push({ id, src, title, type }); saveAndRenderQueue();
            Swal.fire({ title: '已加入', icon: 'success', toast: true, position: 'top-end', timer: 1000, showConfirmButton: false });
        }
        function toggleQueueUI() { $("#queueUI").toggleClass("active"); }
        function saveAndRenderQueue() { localStorage.setItem('dbPlayQueue', JSON.stringify(playQueue)); renderQueue(); }
        function renderQueue() {
            $("#queueCount").text(playQueue.length);
            $("#queueList").html(playQueue.map((item, idx) => `<div class="queue-item"><span>${idx + 1}. ${item.title}</span><i class="bi bi-x-circle text-danger" onclick="playQueue.splice(${idx},1);saveAndRenderQueue()"></i></div>`).join('') || '<div class="p-2 text-muted text-center small">無待播歌曲</div>');
        }
        function doSearch() { config.searchKey = $("#inputSearch").val(); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function changePageSize() { config.pageSize = parseInt($("#pageSizeSelect").val()); config.pageIndex = 1; loadGrid(); }
        function setSort(col) { config.sortBy = col; config.sortDir = (config.sortDir === "DESC" ? "ASC" : "DESC"); loadGrid(); }
        function openAddModal() { $("#modalTitle").text("新增"); $("#editId").val(""); $("#fieldName").val(""); $("#fieldContent").val(""); $("#fieldMail").val("3"); $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('InsertData')"); dataModal.show(); }
        function openEdit(id, n, m, c) { $("#modalTitle").text("修改 #"+id); $("#editId").val(id); $("#fieldName").val(n); $("#fieldMail").val(m); $("#fieldContent").val(c); $("#btnSubmit").attr("onclick", "submitData('UpdateData')"); dataModal.show(); }
        function submitData(m) {
            let d = { id: $("#editId").val() || 0, name: $("#fieldName").val(), mail: $("#fieldMail").val(), content: $("#fieldContent").val() };
            $.ajax({ type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/" + m, data: JSON.stringify(d), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8", success: function () { dataModal.hide(); loadGrid(); } });
        }
        function askDelete(id) {
            Swal.fire({ title: '確認刪除？', icon: 'warning', input: 'password', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) $.ajax({ type: "POST", url: SERVER_URL + "/DeleteData", data: JSON.stringify({ id: id, pwd: res.value }), contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8", success: function (r) { if (r.d.success) loadGrid(); else Swal.fire("錯誤", r.d.msg, "error"); } });
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
--- ### 第二部分：後端程式 `message5.aspx.vb`
vbnet
Imports System.Data.SqlClient
Imports System.Web.Services
Imports System.Web.Script.Services

Public Class message5
    Inherits System.Web.UI.Page
    Private Shared ReadOnly ConnString As String = System.Web.Configuration.WebConfigurationManager.ConnectionStrings("fromaccessdatabaseConnectionString").ConnectionString

    Protected Sub Page_Load(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles Me.Load
        Response.AddHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        Response.AddHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, GET, OPTIONS")
        Response.AddHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
        If Request.HttpMethod = "OPTIONS" Then Response.End()
    End Sub

    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function GetData(ByVal searchKey As String, ByVal sortBy As String, ByVal sortDir As String, ByVal pageIndex As Integer, ByVal pageSize As Integer) As Object
        Try
            Dim rows As New List(Of Dictionary(Of String, Object))()
            Dim totalCount As Integer = 0
            Dim dbCol As String = If(sortBy = "姓名", "name1", If(sortBy = "郵件", "mail1", If(sortBy = "內容", "word1", If(sortBy = "日期", "時間戳記", "識別碼"))))
            Dim finalKey As String = "%" & (searchKey & "").Trim() & "%"

            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                conn.Open()
                Dim countSql As String = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key)"
                Dim countCmd As New SqlCommand(countSql, conn)
                countCmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
                totalCount = CInt(countCmd.ExecuteScalar())

                Dim sql As String = ""
                If pageSize >= 1000000 Then
                    sql = String.Format("SELECT 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir)
                Else
                    Dim startRow As Integer = (pageIndex - 1) * pageSize
                    sql = String.Format("SELECT TOP {2} 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容, 時間戳記 as 日期 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) AND 識別碼 NOT IN (SELECT TOP {3} 識別碼 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE (name1 LIKE @key OR mail1 LIKE @key OR word1 LIKE @key) ORDER BY {0} {1}) ORDER BY {0} {1}", dbCol, sortDir, pageSize, startRow)
                End If

                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@key", SqlDbType.NVarChar).Value = finalKey
                Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
                Dim dt As New DataTable()
                da.Fill(dt)
                For Each dr As DataRow In dt.Rows
                    Dim row As New Dictionary(Of String, Object)()
                    For Each col As DataColumn In dt.Columns
                        row.Add(col.ColumnName, dr(col).ToString())
                    Next
                    rows.Add(row)
                Next
            End Using
            Return New With {.data = rows, .totalCount = totalCount}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.error = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()>
    <ScriptMethod(ResponseFormat:=ResponseFormat.Json)>
    Public Shared Function GetSlotNames() As Object
        Dim list As New List(Of Object)()
        Try
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT SlotID, SlotName FROM PlaylistSlots ORDER BY SlotID", conn)
                conn.Open()
                Dim dr As SqlDataReader = cmd.ExecuteReader()
                While dr.Read()
                    list.Add(New With {.id = dr("SlotID"), .name = dr("SlotName").ToString()})
                End While
            End Using
        Catch : End Try
        Return list
    End Function

    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function SavePlaylist(ByVal slotId As Integer, ByVal slotName As String, ByVal ids As String) As Object
        Try
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim sql As String = "UPDATE PlaylistSlots SET SlotName=@name, IDList=@ids, UpdateTime=GETDATE() WHERE SlotID=@sid"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", slotName)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ids", ids)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sid", slotId)
                conn.Open() : cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End Using
            Return New With {.success = True}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.success = False, .msg = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function LoadPlaylistItems(ByVal slotId As Integer) As Object
        Try
            Dim ids As String = ""
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT IDList FROM PlaylistSlots WHERE SlotID=@sid", conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sid", slotId)
                conn.Open()
                ids = cmd.ExecuteScalar()?.ToString()
            End Using
            If String.IsNullOrEmpty(ids) Then Return New With {.data = New List(Of Object)()}
            
            Dim rows As New List(Of Dictionary(Of String, Object))()
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim sql As String = "SELECT 識別碼, name1 as 姓名, mail1 as 郵件, word1 as 內容 FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE 識別碼 IN (" & ids & ") ORDER BY CHARINDEX(',' + CAST(識別碼 AS VARCHAR) + ',', ',' + @orderIds + ',')"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@orderIds", ids)
                conn.Open()
                Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
                Dim dt As New DataTable()
                da.Fill(dt)
                For Each dr As DataRow In dt.Rows
                    Dim row As New Dictionary(Of String, Object)()
                    For Each col As DataColumn In dt.Columns
                        row.Add(col.ColumnName, dr(col).ToString())
                    Next
                    rows.Add(row)
                Next
            End Using
            Return New With {.data = rows}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.error = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function

    ' 新增、修改、刪除的方法保持原樣即可...
    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function InsertData(ByVal name As String, ByVal mail As String, ByVal content As String) As Object
        Try
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim sql As String = "INSERT INTO guestinfowd15 (name1, mail1, word1, 時間戳記) VALUES (@name, @mail, @content, GETDATE())"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", name)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@mail", mail)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@content", content)
                conn.Open() : cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End Using
            Return New With {.success = True}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.success = False, .msg = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function UpdateData(ByVal id As Integer, ByVal name As String, ByVal mail As String, ByVal content As String) As Object
        Try
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim sql As String = "UPDATE guestinfowd15 SET name1=@name, mail1=@mail, word1=@content WHERE 識別碼=@id"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", name)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@mail", mail)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@content", content)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", id)
                conn.Open() : cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End Using
            Return New With {.success = True}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.success = False, .msg = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()>
    Public Shared Function DeleteData(ByVal id As Integer, ByVal pwd As String) As Object
        If pwd <> "*****" Then Return New With {.success = False, .msg = "密碼錯誤！"}
        Try
            Using conn As New SqlConnection(ConnString)
                Dim sql As String = "DELETE FROM guestinfowd15 WHERE 識別碼 = @id"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sql, conn)
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", id)
                conn.Open() : cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End Using
            Return New With {.success = True}
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New With {.success = False, .msg = ex.Message}
        End Try
    End Function
End Class
### 檢視建議： 1. 資料表一致性：後端程式碼現在統一使用 `guestinfowd15`，請確認這與您的實體 SQL 資料表名稱一致。 2. MIME 設定：在電腦版 IIS 運行時，若 `.m4a` 仍報 404，請在 `Web.config` 中加入 `<mimeMap fileExtension=".m4a" mimeType="audio/mp4" />`。 3. JSON 長度：若「全部顯示」導致 500 錯誤，請在 `Web.config` 的 `jsonSerialization` 中調大 `maxJsonLength`。 您可以直接部署這組檔案，它是目前針對您需求最穩定的版本。

🤖 GEMINI
這兩項問題分別涉及 CSS 層級 (z-index) 以及 JavaScript 路徑處理邏輯。 在電腦版 IIS 環境下，路徑的斜線與編碼非常敏感。以下是針對這兩個問題的修復方案： ### 1. 解決「存入此位」訊息被擋住 這是因為置頂播放器的 `z-index` 設定為 `1100`，而 SweetAlert2 (彈出訊息) 預設層級通常在 `1060` 左右。我們需要把播放器的層級調低，並確保彈出視窗比它高。 ### 2. 解決 404 錯誤 (修正路徑與編碼) 錯誤訊息顯示網址中 遺失了 `vedio1/` 前綴，導致瀏覽器去根目錄找檔案。這通常是因為 `type` (mail1) 判斷失效或變數傳遞錯誤。另外，檔名中的 空格 在 IIS 環境下需要精確處理。 --- ### 請更新 `message5.html` 中的以下區塊： #### A. 修正 CSS (層級問題) 請找到 `<style>` 區塊，修改 `.player-sticky-zone` 的 `z-index`：

#### B. 修正 JavaScript 路徑產生器 (404 問題) 請找到 `getFinalSrc` 與 `handleSmartPlay` 函數，使用以下更強壯的版本：
javascript
function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
    if (type == "4" || (rawPath && rawPath.startsWith('http'))) return rawPath;

    // 1. 處理原始路徑：先解碼回中文，避免重複編碼問題
    let decodedPath = "";
    try {
        decodedPath = decodeURIComponent(rawPath);
    } catch (e) {
        decodedPath = rawPath;
    }

    // 2. 擷取純檔名 (處理 Windows 反斜線或網頁斜線)
    let fileName = decodedPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/').split('/').pop(); 

    // 3. 判斷資料夾前綴 (1, 3 為虛擬目錄 vedio1; 2 為實體 vedio)
    // 加上安全檢查，如果 type 是 undefined 或空，預設給 3
    let folderType = String(type || "3").trim();
    let folder = (folderType === "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";
    
    // 4. 組合最終網址 (使用 encodeURIComponent 處理空格與特殊字元)
    let finalURL = folder + encodeURIComponent(fileName);
    
    console.log("Debug - 來源類型:", folderType, "產出網址:", finalURL);
    return finalURL;
}

// 修改後的播放函數
function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn, id) {
    // 標示目前播放列
    $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
    if (btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");

    const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
    const titleDisplay = document.getElementById('nowPlayingTitle');

    // 取得修正後的路徑
    let finalURL = getFinalSrc(rawPath, type);

    if (type == "4") {
        titleDisplay.innerText = "📺 開啟 YouTube: " + title;
        window.open(finalURL, '_blank');
        return;
    }

    // 更新播放器來源
    player.src = finalURL;
    player.load();
    
    let playPromise = player.play();
    if (playPromise !== undefined) {
        playPromise.then(() => {
            $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("▶ 正在播放: " + title);
        }).catch(error => {
            console.log("自動播放被攔截，等待點擊");
            $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("⚠️ 請點擊播放鈕啟動：" + title);
        });
    }
}
#### C. 修正「下一首」邏輯中的參數傳遞 確保 `playNext` 抓取下一首時，能正確帶入 `mail1` (類型) 參數：
javascript
function playNext() {
    if (playQueue.length > 0) {
        const next = playQueue.shift();
        saveAndRenderQueue();
        handleSmartPlay(next.src, next.title, next.type, null, next.id);
    } else {
        // 找尋清單中的下一列
        let nextRow = $(".active-row").next("tr");
        if (!nextRow.length) {
            // 如果沒選中任何一列，則播第一列
            nextRow = $("#dataTableBody tr:first");
        }
        
        if (nextRow.length) {
            // 模擬點擊播放按鈕 (這會觸發 handleSmartPlay 並帶入正確參數)
            nextRow.find(".btn-play").click();
            nextRow[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        } else {
            Swal.fire({ title: "播完了", icon: "info", zIndex: 2000 });
        }
    }
}
--- ### 💡 為什麼這樣改會生效？ 1. Z-Index 衝突：將播放器設為 `1000`，而訊息視窗維持在 `1060+`。這樣訊息就不會被黑色的影片區域遮住了。 2. 404 錯誤 (飛蘭.mp4)：檔名中的空格 `飞兰 我只...` 在網址中必須是 `%20`。我改寫的 `getFinalSrc` 會強制先抓出「檔名」，再重新執行 `encodeURIComponent`，這能保證路徑絕對會帶上 `vedio1/` 前綴，且空格編碼正確。 3. 類型防呆：加入了 `String(type || "3").trim()`，防止資料庫中 `mail1` 欄位若是 null 或多餘空白導致路徑判斷失敗。 ### 建議操作： 1. 更新 HTML 代碼。 2. 重新整理網頁。 3. 測試「存入此位」，看訊息是否浮現在播放器上方。 4. 點擊「飛蘭」那首歌，觀察 F12 控制台 輸出的 `Debug - 產出網址` 是否正確包含 `vedio1/`。

這兩類問題主要導因於 IIS 伺服器對特殊字元的過濾機制 以及 置頂元素對彈出視窗的層級壓制。 以下是針對您提供的報錯訊息進行的深度修正： ### 1. 解決 404 錯誤：特殊字元（如 `~`）處理 報錯顯示 `高勝美 ~ 緣.mp4` 找不到。在 IIS 中，檔名包含 波浪號 (`~`)、空格 或 特殊符號 時，如果編碼方式不完全符合 IIS 的「要求篩選」規則，就會報 404。 修正邏輯： 我們不再對整個檔名進行過度編碼，而是採用最標準的轉義方式，並確保路徑前後沒有隱藏的空白字元。 ### 2. 解決訊息被擋住：層級（z-index）終極修正 我們將播放器的層級降到最低（但仍高於表格），並強制 Bootstrap 的 Modal 與 SweetAlert2 置於最高層。 --- ### 請將 `message5.html` 的內容更新為此版本：

    <script>
        const SERVER_URL = "message5.aspx";
        var config = { searchKey: "", sortBy: "識別碼", sortDir: "DESC", pageIndex: 1, pageSize: 10000000 };
        var playQueue = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dbPlayQueue')) || [];
        var dataModal;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            dataModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('dataModal'));
            loadSlots();
            loadGrid();
        });

        // --- 修正路徑產生器 (特別針對 IIS 特殊符號) ---
        function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
            if (type == "4" || rawPath.startsWith('http')) return rawPath.trim();
            
            // 1. 清理路徑並抓出檔名
            let cleanPath = decodeURIComponent(rawPath).trim();
            let fileName = cleanPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/').split('/').pop();

            // 2. 決定目錄
            let folder = (type == "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";

            // 3. 使用較溫和的編碼 (保留 ~ 等 IIS 可能需要的符號)
            // 如果您的 IIS 還是報 404，請嘗試直接使用 fileName 而不編碼
            return folder + encodeURIComponent(fileName).replace(/%20/g, ' '); 
        }

        function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn) {
            $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
            if (btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");

            if (type == "4") { window.open(rawPath.trim(), '_blank'); return; }

            const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');

javascript
// 強化版路徑產生器
function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
    if (!rawPath) return "";
    if (type == "4" || rawPath.toLowerCase().startsWith('http')) return rawPath.trim();

    try {
        // 1. 先解碼，確保拿到原始中文檔名
        let decodedPath = decodeURIComponent(rawPath).trim();
        
        // 2. 只取最後的檔案名稱 (處理 \ 或 /)
        let fileName = decodedPath.split(/[\\\/]/).pop();

        // 3. 強制判斷資料夾 (1, 3 走虛擬; 2 走實體)
        let folder = (String(type) === "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";

        // 4. 重新編碼。注意：IIS 有時不喜歡過度編碼，這裡用 encodeURI
        let finalURL = folder + encodeURI(fileName);

        console.log("播放調試 -> 類型:" + type + " | 原始:" + rawPath + " | 最終:" + finalURL);
        return finalURL;
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("路徑處理出錯", e);
        return (type == "2" ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/") + rawPath;
    }
}

// 修正播放函數
function handleSmartPlay(rawPath, title, type, btn, id) {
    $(".table tr").removeClass("active-row");
    if (btn) $(btn).closest("tr").addClass("active-row");

    const player = document.getElementById('mainPlayer');
    const titleDisplay = document.getElementById('nowPlayingTitle');
    
    let finalURL = getFinalSrc(rawPath, type);

    if (String(type) === "4") {
        titleDisplay.innerText = "📺 YouTube: " + title;
        window.open(finalURL, '_blank');
        return;
    }

    player.src = finalURL;
    player.load();
    player.play().then(() => {
        $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("▶ 正在播放: " + title);
    }).catch(error => {
        $("#nowPlayingTitle").text("⚠️ 點擊播放鈕啟動：" + title);
        console.error("播放攔截或404:", finalURL);
    });
}

// 修正下一首邏輯 (確保抓得到正確的 type)
function playNext() {
    if (playQueue.length > 0) {
        const next = playQueue.shift();
        saveAndRenderQueue();
        handleSmartPlay(next.src, next.title, next.type, null, next.id);
    } else {
        // 找尋清單中的下一列
        let nextRow = $(".active-row").next("tr");
        if (!nextRow.length) nextRow = $("#dataTableBody tr:first");
        
        if (nextRow.length) {
            // 這裡直接模擬點擊該行的播放按鈕，這樣參數最準確
            nextRow.find(".btn-play").click();
            nextRow[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        }
    }
}

Investigating Filename Encoding I'm now diving into a suspected encoding issue. The player attempts to load a file with a complex name, `vedio1/火影忍者疾風傳...Blue Bird.mp4`. The URL construction seems correct, but IIS might be the culprit. I'm focusing on URL length and character restrictions, specifically the Chinese characters, full-width parentheses, spaces, and hyphens. Double-encoding and IIS "Request Filtering" are also suspects. 🤖 GEMINI
這次報錯的檔案名稱包含：長中文字、全形括號 `（ ）`、空格、以及多個符號。這在電腦版 IIS 伺服器上是一個非常經典的難題。 即便檔案存在，IIS 常因為「安全過濾」認為網址包含特殊字元而直接回絕（報 404），或是因為 Windows 路徑長度限制導致虛擬目錄抓不到檔案。 請依照以下三個層次進行修正： ### 1. 伺服器端修正：開啟「允許雙重轉義」(最強效) IIS 預設會阻擋網址中出現 `%` 或特殊編碼。請在您 PC 執行網頁的資料夾 中，開啟或建立 `web.config`，務必加入這段：
xml
<configuration>
    <system.webServer>
        <security>
            <requestFiltering allowDoubleEscaping="true">
                <!-- 放大網址與長度限制 -->
                <requestLimits maxAllowedContentLength="2147483647" maxUrl="4096" maxQueryString="2048" />
            </requestFiltering>
        </security>
        <staticContent>
            <!-- 確保播放格式被認得 -->
            <remove fileExtension=".mp4" />
            <mimeMap fileExtension=".mp4" mimeType="video/mp4" />
        </staticContent>
    </system.webServer>
</configuration>
--- ### 2. 前端 JavaScript 修正：優化編碼邏輯 有些瀏覽器會自動對網址編碼一次，加上我們程式編碼，會變成「雙重編碼」。我優化了 `getFinalSrc`，讓它產出的路徑更符合 IIS 的胃口。 請替換 `message5.html` 中的 `getFinalSrc` 函數：
javascript
function getFinalSrc(rawPath, type) {
    if (!rawPath) return "";
    // 如果是 YouTube 或已經是 http 開頭則不處理
    if (String(type) === "4" || rawPath.toLowerCase().startsWith('http')) return rawPath.trim();

    try {
        // 1. 取得純檔名 (移除路徑前綴)
        let decodedPath = decodeURIComponent(rawPath).trim();
        let fileName = decodedPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/').split('/').pop();

        // 2. 決定目錄 (1,3 -> vedio1/; 2 -> vedio/)
        let folder = (String(type) === "2") ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/";

        // 3. IIS 最佳編碼策略：使用 encodeURI 而非 encodeURIComponent
        // 因為 encodeURI 不會轉義 / : 等符號，且對中文支援較好
        let finalURL = folder + encodeURI(fileName);

        console.log("IIS 播放路徑檢查 -> ", finalURL);
        return finalURL;
    } catch (e) {
        return (String(type) === "2" ? "vedio/" : "vedio1/") + rawPath;
    }
}
--- ### 3. 實體檔案檢查：路徑長度限制 如果做了上述兩點，「火影忍者...」這首歌還是 404，請檢查： 1. 檔案命名過長：Windows 系統（IIS 跑在 Windows 上）對檔案完整路徑有 260 個字元 的限制。 * 您的檔名非常長（約 100 字元）。 * 如果 NAS 原始路徑是 `\\weizen_ds215\資料區只能讀取\youtube影片重要\....`。 * 解決方法：請嘗試在 File Station 裡，把這個檔案名稱改短，例如改為 `火影忍者-青鳥.mp4`，然後在留言板管理介面也同步修改「路徑(word1)」為 `火影忍者-青鳥.mp4`。通常改短後 404 就會消失。 2. 全形符號：檔名中的 `（` `）` 建議改為半形 `(` `)`，因為 IIS 在處理網路路徑（UNC Path）時，全形符號偶爾會發生解析錯誤。 ### 💡 建議測試順序： 1. 先改 `web.config` (這最關鍵，通常改完這個就好了)。 2. 若不行，修改 JS 代碼。 3. 若還是 404，將該影片檔案名稱改短測試。 請問您修改 `web.config` 並開啟 `allowDoubleEscaping` 後，長檔名的影片可以播了嗎？
